Objective\\r\\n\\r\\nThe latest Creator Evidence Review concluded **REVISE**.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe remaining hypothesis is:\\r\\n\\r\\n> After a documentary script changes, a small production team may need to re-check whether the revised claims still match the correct interview wording, external primary documents, archive assets, dates, and current source versions before picture lock / publication.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis task must determine whether that workflow is:\\r\\n\\r\\n1. real and recurrent,\\r\\n2. sufficiently costly or error-prone to matter,\\r\\n3. not already handled cleanly by Trint / Axle / Frame.io / MAM / current editorial workflows,\\r\\n4. not trivially replaced by a file-aware ChatGPT / Claude workflow using the same authorized folder,\\r\\n5. plausibly improved by a NAS-resident Evidence Review workflow,\\r\\n6. and suitable for a clear <3-minute competition demo.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe correct outcome may be:\\r\\n- **GO**\\r\\n- **REVISE**\\r\\n- **DROP**\\r\\n\\r\\nA DROP result is acceptable.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 2. Required context to read first\\r\\n\\r\\nRead in full:\\r\\n\\r\\n1. `docs/09_COMPETITION_INTELLIGENCE.md`\\r\\n2. `docs/TOP8_TECH_FEASIBILITY_2026-11-01.md`\\r\\n3. `docs/VERTICAL_WORKFLOW_DISCOVERY_2026-11-01.md`\\r\\n4. `docs/EVIDENCE_FIRST_POSITIONING_2026-11-01.md`\\r\\n5. `docs/NAS_NATIVE_VALUE_VALIDATION_2026-11-01.md`\\r\\n6. `docs/CREATOR_EVIDENCE_REVIEW_VALIDATION_2026-11-01.md`\\r\\n7. `docs/00_PROJECT_STATUS.md`\\r\\n\\r\\nPreserve all established limitations.\\r\\n\\r\\nImportant current conclusions:\\r\\n- SourceWeave 2.0 is not yet validated.\\r\\n- The current Hero is specifically **documentary pre-lock source review after a script change**.\\r\\n- General AI can probably reproduce much of the content reasoning on the same small packet.\\r\\n- NAS is not automatically necessary.\\r\\n- Trint / Axle / Frame.io and other media tools are strong incumbent pressure.\\r\\n- The possible residual gap is repeated cross-source re-checking across the latest script, interview transcript/audio references, external PDFs, archive stills/assets and source versions.\\r\\n- No video frame extraction/editing is available in the public UGREEN capability model.\\r\\n- No arbitrary Python/Docker/backend/general HTTP should be assumed.\\r\\n- Human editorial judgment remains final.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 3. Validation target\\r\\n\\r\\nValidate this exact workflow:\\r\\n\\r\\n### Trigger\\r\\nA documentary script has changed shortly before picture lock / final editorial approval.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Reviewer needs to re-check\\r\\n- whether revised factual claims still match the exact interview/source wording,\\r\\n- whether the cited source is still the current/approved version,\\r\\n- whether an archival asset ID still corresponds to the intended source,\\r\\n- whether different sources now conflict,\\r\\n- whether a source is unreadable or unavailable,\\r\\n- whether a claim became broader than the evidence actually supports.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Proposed SourceWeave 2.0 output\\r\\nFor each revised claim:\\r\\n- CLAIM\\r\\n- SUPPORTING\\r\\n- CONTRADICTING\\r\\n- SOURCE LIMITATION\\r\\n- UNREAD / FAILED SOURCE\\r\\n- VERSION / ASSET ID ISSUE\\r\\n- UNCERTAIN\\r\\n- HUMAN DISPOSITION\\r\\n- exact source pointer where genuinely available\\r\\n\\r\\nDo not claim truth, legal clearance, rights clearance, journalistic approval, or automatic publication authority.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 4. Real-workflow evidence gate\\r\\n\\r\\nThis task must first establish whether the workflow is real.\\r\\n\\r\\nUse current public evidence from sources such as:\\r\\n- documentary fact-checking / archival-production guidance\\r\\n- broadcaster/editorial standards\\r\\n- documentary production training\\r\\n- archival producer workflows\\r\\n- post-production/editorial workflows\\r\\n- practitioner discussions where appropriate\\r\\n- Trint / Axle / Frame.io / MAM documentation\\r\\n\\r\\nFind evidence for as many as possible:\\r\\n1. script assertions are checked against sources,\\r\\n2. checks happen again after revisions / edits,\\r\\n3. source quotations / page / timestamp / asset references matter,\\r\\n4. revisions can invalidate earlier checking,\\r\\n5. external documents and archival assets sit outside one transcript tool,\\r\\n6. a human checker/editor retains final authority.\\r\\n\\r\\nFor each claim label:\\r\\n- FACT\\r\\n- INFERENCE\\r\\n- ASSUMPTION\\r\\n- UNKNOWN\\r\\n\\r\\n### Hard rule\\r\\nIf no credible evidence can establish a recurring **post-revision / pre-lock re-check** job, do not synthesize one from generic fact-checking.\\r\\n\\r\\nReturn **DROP** or **REVISE — workflow not directly established**.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 5. Incumbent workflow reconstruction\\r\\n\\r\\nReconstruct how a competent small documentary team could handle the job today.\\r\\n\\r\\nAt minimum compare:\\r\\n- Trint\\r\\n- Axle / MAM-style media management\\r\\n- Frame.io or equivalent review workflow\\r\\n- spreadsheets / source sheets / editorial checklists\\r\\n- shared folder / Dropbox / Drive / NAS\\r\\n- file-aware ChatGPT / Claude where applicable\\r\\n\\r\\nFor each tool/workflow answer:\\r\\n1. What part of the job is already solved?\\r\\n2. What source types are handled?\\r\\n3. What revision/history support exists?\\r\\n4. Can it link back to exact media/source context?\\r\\n5. Can it compare the latest script against multiple external sources?\\r\\n6. Can it show unread/failed inputs?\\r\\n7. Can it preserve reviewer disposition?\\r\\n8. What manual work remains?\\r\\n\\r\\nDo not create a gap by deliberately omitting normal incumbent features.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 6. Fair baseline tests\\r\\n\\r\\n### A. Same-folder General-AI Test\\r\\nAssume a capable file-aware ChatGPT / Claude can access the same authorized project folder.\\r\\n\\r\\nGive it the same:\\r\\n- latest script,\\r\\n- prior checked script,\\r\\n- interview transcript,\\r\\n- source PDF,\\r\\n- archive metadata sheet,\\r\\n- asset index,\\r\\n- source checklist,\\r\\n- output instructions.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk whether it can:\\r\\n- identify changed claims,\\r\\n- compare them to sources,\\r\\n- surface contradictions,\\r\\n- mark uncertainty,\\r\\n- produce a source-linked review table.\\r\\n\\r\\nDo not handicap the baseline.\\r\\n\\r\\nThen identify what SourceWeave retains that is operationally meaningful.\\r\\n\\r\\nIf the answer is only:\\r\\n- prewritten prompt,\\r\\n- prettier table,\\r\\n- four status labels,\\r\\n- saved Markdown,\\r\\n\\r\\nthe differentiation fails.\\r\\n\\r\\n### B. Trint + MAM Test\\r\\nAssume the team correctly uses Trint plus Axle/another MAM and a normal review tool.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk:\\r\\n- what exact step still requires repeated manual cross-source reconciliation?\\r\\n- is that step recurrent?\\r\\n- does NAS access reduce real preparation work?\\r\\n\\r\\nIf no material residual remains, downgrade or DROP.\\r\\n\\r\\n### C. NAS Removal Test\\r\\nReplace NAS with:\\r\\n- Dropbox / Drive,\\r\\n- SharePoint,\\r\\n- mounted local/shared folder,\\r\\n- MAM-managed storage.\\r\\n\\r\\nWhat meaningful advantage disappears?\\r\\n\\r\\nValid answers must be operational, e.g.:\\r\\n- large originals already live on NAS,\\r\\n- repeated re-check over long-lived source corpus,\\r\\n- no duplicate export/copy step,\\r\\n- stable team source location,\\r\\n- cross-project historical archive.\\r\\n\\r\\nInvalid answer:\\r\\n- \\\"because the files are on NAS.\\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n## 7. Representative packet validation\\r\\n\\r\\nIf public evidence supports the workflow, define one **representative synthetic packet** grounded in the documented practice.\\r\\n\\r\\nDo not call it a real customer packet.\\r\\n\\r\\nUse no more than 8 files, for example:\\r\\n1. `script-v12.txt` — previous checked script\\r\\n2. `script-v13.txt` — revised script with 2 changed claims\\r\\n3. `interview-transcript.txt` — source wording\\r\\n4. `primary-source.pdf` — date / factual record\\r\\n5. `archive-index.csv` or readable text equivalent — asset ID and date metadata\\r\\n6. `source-note.txt` — prior checker note\\r\\n7. `clean-control.pdf` — one claim that still matches\\r\\n8. `unread-source.pdf` — intentionally unreadable fixture\\r\\n\\r\\nPlant exactly:\\r\\n- one revision that overstates a source,\\r\\n- one date/source conflict,\\r\\n- one clean control,\\r\\n- one unread / uncertain dependency.\\r\\n\\r\\nDo not require unsupported video-frame extraction.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 8. Workflow comparison experiment design\\r\\n\\r\\nDesign, but do not falsely report as executed unless the environment genuinely allows it, a fair comparison between:\\r\\n\\r\\n### Arm 1 — Manual / current workflow\\r\\nSource sheet + transcript/MAM/review tools.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Arm 2 — File-aware general AI\\r\\nSame authorized packet + strong prompt.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Arm 3 — Proposed SourceWeave 2.0\\r\\nSame packet + fixed review contract.\\r\\n\\r\\nCompare:\\r\\n- setup/preparation steps\\r\\n- source-selection burden\\r\\n- stale-version risk\\r\\n- changed-claim detection\\r\\n- source pointer fidelity\\r\\n- unread-source visibility\\r\\n- repeated rerun after one file changes\\r\\n- human-review clarity\\r\\n- saved output traceability\\r\\n\\r\\nNo invented time savings.\\r\\nNo fabricated accuracy percentages.\\r\\n\\r\\nIf no actual execution is possible, clearly mark the comparison as **prospective test design**.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 9. Hero demo gate\\r\\n\\r\\nDefine one <=180-second demo only if the workflow survives.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe demo must show:\\r\\n1. latest script revision,\\r\\n2. exact changed claim,\\r\\n3. one source that supports a narrower statement,\\r\\n4. one conflict or source/version issue,\\r\\n5. one clean control,\\r\\n6. one unread/uncertain case,\\r\\n7. human refuses to lock/publish until reviewed.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe visual story must be understandable without explaining documentary production for more than ~20 seconds.\\r\\n\\r\\nDo not create the demo in this task.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 10. Decision framework\\r\\n\\r\\nReturn exactly one:\\r\\n\\r\\n### GO\\r\\nUse only if evidence supports all of:\\r\\n- recurring real workflow,\\r\\n- credible NAS-resident target cohort,\\r\\n- material residual after correct incumbent use,\\r\\n- meaningful operational advantage over same-folder general AI,\\r\\n- feasible UGREEN input types,\\r\\n- clear 3-minute demo.\\r\\n\\r\\n### REVISE\\r\\nUse if:\\r\\n- workflow is real,\\r\\n- but NAS advantage or residual gap remains unproven,\\r\\n- and one specific bounded question could still resolve it.\\r\\n\\r\\n### DROP\\r\\nUse if:\\r\\n- post-revision re-check is not directly established,\\r\\n- incumbent tools already remove the meaningful job,\\r\\n- general AI removes the operational advantage,\\r\\n- or the remaining demo is merely file comparison / RAG formatting.\\r\\n\\r\\nDo not keep REVISE indefinitely. 英文字**（隊伍同意聲明、成員姓名、電子簽名、第三方授權資訊不計）。十題（Devpost 原文摘要）：\\r\\n   ①一句話說明 Skill；②目標使用者與問題；③解法；④主要 user flow；⑤**與現有工具或一般 AI 方案的差異**；⑥使用哪些 UGREEN AI NAS 功能、在哪一步；⑦技術做法（workflow、CLI、expert agent 編排、輸入、權限、模型選擇、資料處理）；⑧已完成／未完成／待 NAS 驗證；⑨決賽測試計畫（**2 個核心測試＋1 個邊界測試**）；⑩已知限制、風險、外部依賴。另須揭露第三方元件／來源／授權。\\r\\n2. **Pre-test Skill Package ZIP**：依 Uliya Skill Development Standard；ZIP 與解壓後各 ≤10 MB、單檔 ≤1 MB、≤100 檔；**禁止 manifest、scripts、executables、binaries**；不得含真實憑證、個資、API key。不需可安裝、不需 NAS 執行證據、不需 repo/影片。\\r\\n3. 可沿用賽前構想，但 **Skill 與核心 workflow 必須在賽期內新開發**；不得重用既有 Skill。\\r\\n\\r\\n**Round 2 提交（FACT S1 S2）**：同一個 Skill 的最終 ZIP（同規格）、**≤3 分鐘公開 YouTube 影片**（英文旁白或完整英文字幕），**必須在指定測試日於指定 NAS 上錄製**實際運作。Round 2 必須建立在 Round 1 同一專案上。\\r\\n\\r\\n**已知官方資訊衝突（FACT，需記錄）**\\r\\n- 賽道名稱：Rules／Devpost 首頁／ai.ugreen.com 為 **Bring Memories to Life**、**Open Innovation**；僅 Devpost gallery 篩選器出現 \\\"Understand Your Life\\\"、\\\"Explore Your Imagination\\\"（S1 S2 S3 S4）。**以 Rules 為準**；任務簡述中的名稱即來自篩選器。\\r\\n- Round 1 影片：ai.ugreen.com 時間軸列 \\\"Prototype Walkthrough Video\\\"，但 Rules、Devpost、同頁 FAQ 皆稱 Round 1 不需影片（S1 S2 S3）。以 Rules 為準。\\r\\n- Starter Kit 建議放 `README.md`，但官方 checker 只允許根目錄 `SKILL.md` 與 `references/`（S5 S6）。**不要放 README**。\\r\\n\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n## 2. Judging Insights\\r\\n\\r\\n**FACT（S2 Rules）**\\r\\n- Round 1 先 **pass/fail**：是否「合理符合主題」且「合理提出 AI NAS 解法」；通過者才計分。\\r\\n- Round 1 依 PDF＋ZIP、預期功能、NAS 能力規劃與**驗證計畫**評分；**缺 NAS 執行證據不會單獨降分**。\\r\\n- **四項標準等權重（Round 1 與 Round 2 相同）**：\\r\\n  1. **User Value**：明確使用者問題、清楚流程、有用回饋、有意義結果。\\r\\n  2. **Innovation & Differentiation**：與一般 AI 工具及既有方案相比的**實質**新功能／互動／流程。\\r\\n  3. **Technical Quality**：編排是否合理、是否正確使用支援的 CLI 與 expert agents、權限與錯誤處理、與開發階段相稱的可靠性。\\r\\n  4. **AI NAS Fit & Validation**：是否有意義地使用 NAS 能力（本地資料、儲存、運算、隱私等），**「而非只是跑在 NAS 上」**。\\r\\n- **Round 1 分數不帶入 Round 2**；勝負只看 Round 2（實機運作、影片、文件，含是否兌現 Round 1 承諾）。\\r\\n- 平手：依標準列出順序逐項比較（User Value 優先），全平則評審投票。\\r\\n\\r\\n**INFERENCE（判斷）**\\r\\n1. **Round 1 是紙上競賽**：入圍 30/988（約 3%）取決於 500 字 PDF 與 SKILL.md 的說服力與可信度，而非執行。評審要在很短時間內讀懂價值。\\r\\n2. **Technical Quality 在 Round 1 實際上是在評「編排設計品質」**：每一步是否對應真實 catalog action、五種 delegation 狀態（completed / failed / cancelled / waiting_user / waiting_approval）是否都處理、破壞性操作是否先確認、是否明說不支援的步驟。這些可以在紙上做到滿分，也最容易被粗糙的參賽者忽略。\\r\\n3. **Innovation 有兩條路**：計入總分，且單獨決定 2 個 Most Creative 獎。高創新但執行風險高的方案仍有獲獎路徑。\\r\\n4. **\\\"rather than merely running on NAS\\\"** 是明示的扣分條件：只對單一檔案做摘要／翻譯的方案，在 AI NAS Fit 上可預期低分。\\r\\n5. **⑤（差異化）與 ⑨（2 核心＋1 邊界測試）是官方強迫回答的題目**，評審會直接比較。無法清楚回答 ⑤ 的方案等於自動放棄一個等權重分項。\\r\\n6. 「兌現 Round 1 承諾」也列入 Round 2 評估：**Round 1 過度承諾會在 Round 2 變成扣分**。\\r\\n\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n## 3. Track Analysis\\r\\n\\r\\n選一個賽道；**獎項不分賽道**（FACT S1 S3），所以賽道只影響主題契合與評審預期，不影響名額分配（INFERENCE）。\\r\\n\\r\\n| Track | 官方定義重點（FACT S2） | 可用能力（FACT S6） | 撞題風險 | 批判觀察（INFERENCE） |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| **Automate Your Workflow** | 用 file/document/knowledge/system 能力整理內容、擷取資訊、產生摘要、精簡重複工作 | `ug-document`（qa、extract、reconcile、translate、writing）、`ug-knowledge`（collect-evidence、save-text、audio-transcription）、`ug-filesystem`、`file-workflow`（batch-move、batch-del） | **極高** | catalog **沒有排程、觸發、通知**，所以「自動化」只能是使用者發起的一次性批次。題目名稱暗示背景自動化的方案在技術上不成立。文件 Q&A、收據擷取是最擁擠的區塊，SkillHub 也已有官方 \\\"Structured field extraction\\\" Skill（S7 論壇引述，UNVERIFIED）。 |\\r\\n| **Bring Memories to Life** | 對照片、影片、文件做整理／搜尋／擷取文字／摘要／建相簿 | `ug-photo` 為最大 CLI（18 actions：semantic search、figure/object albums、vision-ocr、vision-vlm、create-album 系列、timeline） | **極高** | 內建相簿已有語意搜尋、人臉／場景／物件辨識、AI 去重（S8），單純「自動相簿」與內建功能重疊。差異化通常要靠**跨資料源**（照片＋文件＋knowledge）或**可驗證的敘事輸出**，但後者容易滑向 LLM wrapper。論壇已有人在做 EXIF／timeline 修復（S7）。 |\\r\\n| **Unleash Your Creativity** | 協助創作者搜尋、標記、挑選、整理、審閱媒體 | `ug-photo` search/vision-vlm、`ug-filemgr`（mediainfo、scan-dedupe、tag-*）、`ug-video`/`ug-music`（僅 search/control/字幕） | 中–高 | **沒有任何影像／影片編輯或轉檔能力**（catalog 無 transform/edit）。ai.ugreen.com 活動頁寫「transform」，但 Rules 已改為 \\\"search, tag, select, organize, review\\\"。承諾「修圖／剪輯」不可行。專業創作者已有 Lightroom 等工具，⑤ 的差異化門檻高。 |\\r\\n| **Open Innovation** | 組合 NAS/AI/媒體/檔案/知識能力，解決真實需求 | 全部 6 個 agent，含 **`ug-surveillance`（8 個唯讀 action：event-timeline、event-evidence、faces、vehicle-plates…）** | 較低但不確定 | 變異最大：主題契合度的 pass/fail 門檻是「合理提出 AI NAS 解法」，風險在**說服力**而非規則。surveillance 是使用較少的能力，但**決賽測試環境是否有攝影機與事件資料 UNKNOWN**；人臉、車牌涉及個資，展示與隱私風險高。 |\\r\\n\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n## 4. NAS / UGOS Constraints（決定可行性的硬限制）\\r\\n\\r\\n**Skill 的技術本質（FACT S5 S6，已由 Claude 直接檢查官方 kit v0.7.3 原始碼驗證）**\\r\\n- Skill 是 **Markdown 編排文件**，不是程式。frontmatter 只允許 `name`、`description`；主 Skill 只能用 `delegate --target <agent-id> --instruction '<task>'` 把工作交給已註冊的 expert。\\r\\n- checker 會拒絕：`bash/sh/python/js/ts` 程式碼區塊（`SECURITY_SCRIPT_EXECUTION_UNSUPPORTED`）、`curl`/`wget`（`SECURITY_REMOTE_DOWNLOAD`）、`fetch(`/`axios` 與直接檔案存取（`SECURITY_DIRECT_FILE_ACCESS`）、未註冊 agent（`SECURITY_UNREGISTERED_AGENT`）、寫死的 `/nas`、`/mnt`、`/volume` 路徑。\\r\\n- Rules 原文：「Custom executable scripts, general-purpose programming runtimes, and unauthorized direct access to NAS resources are not supported.」（S2）\\r\\n- 必要章節：`# Goal`、`# Required input`、`# Workflow`（或 `# Orchestration`）、`# Completion and failure`（或 `# Failure handling`）；description 須含觸發語（when / use when）；不得有 TODO/TBD 佔位字。\\r\\n\\r\\n**能力目錄（FACT S6，catalog v2.0.0，2026-09-01；共 9 CLI、6 expert、78 actions）**\\r\\n\\r\\n| Expert | CLI | 重點 |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| file-agent | ug-filesystem / ug-filemgr / file-workflow | 讀取、搜尋、雜湊、mediainfo、scan-dedupe、tag；寫入類（rename、cpmv、del、mkdir、chtimes、batch-move/del）需 gateway 或 workflow approval |\\r\\n| document-agent | ug-document | qa（summary/comparison/extraction/semantic_lookup）、extract、reconcile、metadata；translate、writing 需 approval |\\r\\n| knowledge-agent | ug-knowledge | answer-official（UGREEN 官方說明）、audio-transcription、collect-evidence、save-text |\\r\\n| photo-agent | ug-photo | 相簿、人物／物件相簿、語意搜尋、截圖搜尋、分類、vision-ocr、vision-vlm、create-album 系列、timeline |\\r\\n| security-monitor-agent | ug-surveillance | 全唯讀：cameras、event-timeline、event-evidence、faces、vehicle-plates… |\\r\\n| media-agent | ug-music / ug-video | search、播放控制、字幕搜尋／下載 |\\r\\n\\r\\n**明確不存在的能力（FACT：catalog 中無對應 action）**：排程/cron、事件觸發、通知、Email/IM、任意網路/HTTP、下載（字幕除外）、Docker、備份、使用者/權限管理、App 安裝、影像/影片編輯。官方 kit：「If a required step has no matching capability, state that it is unsupported; never infer support from a product name.」\\r\\n\\r\\n**其他關鍵限制**\\r\\n- **無法在決賽前實測**：官方工具只做靜態檢查，「does not … verify a real device」；「A static pass does not prove upload, installation, compatibility, or device execution」（FACT S6）。沒有模擬器或 mock（INFERENCE）。\\r\\n- **只有一個測試日**（FACT S1 S2），且影片須在當天錄製。\\r\\n- **catalog 品質不足**：每個 action 的 permissions、prerequisites、errorCodes 都是 `\\\"knowledgeStatus\\\": \\\"unknown\\\"`，stability 也是 unknown；部分英文描述是機器翻譯的亂碼（FACT S6）。也就是說，錯誤處理只能依五種 delegation 狀態設計，無法依具體錯誤碼。\\r\\n- **模型**：Rules 鼓勵但不強制用本地模型，可用「properly authorized third-party models」（FACT S2）。但 Skill 本身不能呼叫外部 API（checker 禁止），所以第三方模型只可能是平台層設定（INFERENCE）。本地模型名稱、大小、速度、context 長度皆 UNKNOWN。\\r\\n- **硬體**（官方產品頁，部分內容自相矛盾）：Intel Core Ultra 7 255H（另一處寫 225H）、宣稱 96 TOPS（第三方指出是 CPU+GPU+NPU 合計）、64 GB RAM（FACT S9／第三方 S10）。\\r\\n- **安全與內容規範**（FACT S11 SkillHub Publishing Guidelines）：最小權限、禁 sudo、禁存取 loopback/私網/metadata、禁遠端下載、禁常駐程式、禁 prompt injection 與隱藏指令、**未揭露的資料傳輸一律違規**、須揭露第三方來源。\\r\\n\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n## 5. Collision Risks（撞題風險）\\r\\n\\r\\n**證據強度說明**：Gallery 未公開（FACT S4），網路上找不到公開參賽作品或 repo（FACT S12）。以下排序是 INFERENCE，依據有三：(a) 賽道官方文字直接列出的動詞；(b) catalog 中最顯眼的 action；(c) 公開論壇 4 個技術問題透露的方向（S7）。\\r\\n\\r\\n| 風險 | 類型 | 依據 |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| 極高 | 文件 Q&A／RAG（對 NAS 文件問答、摘要） | `ug-document qa` 是預設用法；內建「Talk directly with your files」（S8） |\\r\\n| 極高 | 收據／發票／財務擷取與對帳 | 論壇直接提問；SkillHub 已有官方 \\\"Structured field extraction\\\"（S7，UNVERIFIED）；內建 OCR 收據搜尋（S8） |\\r\\n| 極高 | 自動相簿／旅行或活動相簿／人物相簿 | 賽道文字＋`create-album`；內建人臉／物件相簿（S8） |\\r\\n| 高 | 自然語言檔案搜尋＋資料夾摘要 | 內建 Universal Search（S8） |\\r\\n| 高 | 照片 EXIF／時間軸修復 | 論壇提問（S7） |\\r\\n| 高 | 檔案整理／重新命名／去重 | `scan-dedupe`；內建 AI 去重（S8） |\\r\\n| 高 | 創作者挑片、標記、culling | 賽道文字 |\\r\\n| 中 | 「那年今日」／年度家庭回顧 | 賽道文字 |\\r\\n| 中 | 會議記錄／週報彙整 | 官方範例即有 meeting notes archive（S6） |\\r\\n| 低–不確定 | 監控事件摘要 | 能力存在，但測試環境資料 UNKNOWN |\\r\\n\\r\\nINFERENCE：988 人中最多 30 名入圍，前四類很可能各有數十件近似方案。在 Innovation 分項（又是 Most Creative 的依據），撞題本身就是扣分。\\r\\n\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n## 6. Common Weak Ideas（常見弱題型）\\r\\n\\r\\n### 6.1 看起來只是 LLM wrapper 的特徵\\r\\n- workflow 實質上只有**一次** `document-agent qa` 或 `photo-agent vision-vlm`，價值全在 prompt 措辭。\\r\\n- 輸出是一般性的文字（摘要、改寫、翻譯、建議），沒有對 NAS 資料做**結構性**的改變或產出（相簿、標籤、歸檔、報告存檔）。\\r\\n- 移除 NAS 後，把同一個檔案貼進任何聊天機器人，可以得到幾乎相同的結果。\\r\\n- 用「智慧」「助理」「AI 管家」包裝，但說不出 ⑤ 的具體差異。\\r\\n- 依賴本地模型的推理品質，而這個能力目前 UNKNOWN（S6）。\\r\\n\\r\\n### 6.2 其實不需要 NAS 的特徵（對應 AI NAS Fit 扣分）\\r\\n- 只處理使用者當下提供的單一檔案，不用到**既有資料庫規模**（全相簿、多年文件、索引、人物／物件辨識結果）。\\r\\n- 不需要本地／隱私優勢：資料本來就適合丟雲端。\\r\\n- 不需要跨資料類型的 join（照片 × 文件 × knowledge × 監控）。\\r\\n- 不留下可在 NAS 上重複使用的產出（save-text 到 knowledge、相簿、標籤）。\\r\\n\\r\\n### 6.3 技術上不成立的常見構想（catalog 限制）\\r\\n- 「自動監控資料夾、定時執行、完成後通知我」：沒有排程、觸發、通知。\\r\\n- 「抓網路資料、串 API、寄 Email、同步雲端」：checker 禁止網路與下載。\\r\\n- 「自動修圖、剪輯、轉檔、產生影片」：沒有編輯或轉檔能力。\\r\\n- 「管理 Docker、備份、使用者權限」：沒有對應能力。\\r\\n- 「附 Python 腳本做處理」：禁止任何 scripts。\\r\\n\\r\\n### 6.4 與內建功能重疊（FACT S8 列出的內建功能）\\r\\n語意照片搜尋、人臉／場景／物件辨識、AI 去重、Universal Search、文件對話、知識庫、OCR 收據搜尋、檔案／語音翻譯、本地語音轉文字。只把其中一項包成 Skill，⑤ 幾乎答不出差異。\\r\\n\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n## 7. High-value Opportunity Areas（方向，不是題目）\\r\\n\\r\\n以下是評分機制與能力限制交集下，**相對較有空間的方向**。每一項都附帶風險，不代表推薦。\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **跨 expert 的資料 join**：價值來自把兩種以上 NAS 資料（例如照片 metadata × 文件內容 × knowledge 證據）連起來，是單一內建功能做不到的。官方範例本身就是多 agent 鏈（S6）。\\r\\n   *風險*：每多一個 agent，未經實測的介面假設就多一個；決賽只有一天。\\r\\n2. **可驗證、可追溯的輸出**：附來源路徑、證據清單、`reconcile` 對帳結果，存成 knowledge 或報告。這呼應 Technical Quality，也符合 cross-hackathon 觀察：贏家常展示 trust mechanism，例如 citations、拒絕臆測、行動前確認（S12，INFERENCE）。\\r\\n   *風險*：很容易滑向「好看的摘要」＝ wrapper。\\r\\n3. **安全的批次整理**：dry-run 報告 → 使用者核准 → 執行 → 結果回報，正面運用 approval 機制，讓 Technical Quality 在紙上就能被看見。\\r\\n   *風險*：gateway approval 是逐次還是可批次核准仍 UNKNOWN（論壇未回覆，S7）；若逐次核准，大批次的使用體驗會崩潰。\\r\\n4. **隱私敏感、使用者不願上雲的本地資料**：直接對應 AI NAS Fit 的「privacy」字眼（S2）。\\r\\n   *風險*：展示時不能用真實個資；影片是**公開** YouTube。\\r\\n5. **使用率較低的能力**：`ug-surveillance` 的唯讀事件證據、`ug-knowledge audio-transcription`、`ug-filemgr` 的 tag／mediainfo 搭配 vision。這些方向撞題機率較低（INFERENCE）。\\r\\n   *風險*：測試環境是否有對應資料（攝影機事件、音檔）UNKNOWN；能力描述是 boilerplate，行為無法事先確認。\\r\\n6. **具體人物 × 具體問題**：cross-hackathon 贏家多半鎖定特定族群與特定痛點，而不是「幫你管理檔案」（S12，INFERENCE；可轉移性中低）。\\r\\n\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n## 8. Red Flags（15 天內可能失敗的技術與流程風險）\\r\\n\\r\\n| # | 風險 | 嚴重度 | 依據 |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| R1 | **決賽前無法實機驗證**：只有靜態 checker；所有 action 行為（格式支援、分頁、命名衝突、輸出結構）都是假設 | 高 | FACT S6；論壇 4 個技術問題都未回覆（S7） |\\r\\n| R2 | **只有一個測試日**，影片也要當天錄；若某個 action 不如預期，幾乎沒有修正時間 | 高 | FACT S1 S2 |\\r\\n| R3 | **Round 2 必須是同一個 Skill**：Round 1 選錯方向就無法換題 | 高 | FACT S2 |\\r\\n| R4 | catalog 的 permissions、errorCodes、stability 全是 unknown，錯誤處理只能泛化設計 | 中 | FACT S6 |\\r\\n| R5 | Approval 摩擦：寫入類 action 需 gateway 核准，批次語意 UNKNOWN | 中–高 | FACT S6；S7 |\\r\\n| R6 | 本地模型能力 UNKNOWN：依賴 vision-vlm、qa 推理品質的方案無法預估 | 中 | S6 S10 |\\r\\n| R7 | 展示資料：測試日要在…9330 tokens truncated…證據／knowledge-agent | `ug-knowledge save-text --path T --content CONTENT --filename-hint N` | state-change | workflow | SUPPORTED | 保存結果不等同 DMS 管理版本。否 |\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Official Track**：Automate Your Workflow。\\r\\n- **AI NAS Fit**：高；比較 NAS 長期散落副本、核准註記與原位來源。\\r\\n- **Capability coverage**：4/8 = **50.0%**（S4/P3/U1/X0）。\\r\\n- **UNKNOWN critical dependencies：6**：V1 完整列舉/權限；V3 格式與完整內容；V4 同族/差異 coverage；V5/V6 來源身份與核准註記綁定；真實頁碼/引用回連；本地模型路由/性能。\\r\\n- **HARD_BLOCKER：No（只對 M 的人核對、註記型版本導航）**。若另承諾真實版本 DAG、自動讀 NAS snapshots 或驗簽，catalog 不支持該擴張。\\r\\n- **Round 1 paper feasibility**：可明說「有來源的候選與比較，不認證真實最終版」；不能沿用市場稿 A 級作已實證結論。\\r\\n- **Round 2 one-day real-device feasibility**：條件式；先證實 PDF/DOCX 能完整讀，再測 10 文件家族與較舊已核准版。任一格式不支援須如實標記未兌現，不臨場隱藏。\\r\\n- **Demo feasibility**：可能；展示較新草稿不被選為已核准版，缺記錄則未知，人選後 manifest 真實落盤。\\r\\n- **Recommended scope reduction**：明確文字格式、少量文件、來源註記與關係表；不做 binary design diff、snapshot 管理、電子簽章／版本 DAG 認證。改掉 DOCX 需求須明說。\\r\\n- **Confidence**：hash/保存對應高；格式與語意比較中低，完整實機可行性未驗證。\\r\\n\\r\\n## 9. ShareSafe（I06）\\r\\n\\r\\n**原核心（M）**：按接收角色，在檢索前確定性 ACL 過濾，之後才取內容／snippet、檢查敏感資料與輸出最小包。M 的 kill 條件明列「無法接可靠 ACL」。不能用 User 手選資料夾冒充角色 ACL adapter。\\r\\n\\r\\n| Step | Workflow／expert | CLI／exact action（C） | R/W | Approval | 分類 | Known／unknown；需要不存在的能力？ |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| S1 | 將接收角色綁到可驗證 principal／file-agent（候選） | `ug-filesystem category --permission V` 僅是 permission filter | read | none；User 提供角色仍非授權證明 | UNKNOWN | V 取值與 principal 語意不明；不能認證接收者身份。未證實 |\\r\\n| S2 | 檢索前算來源與接收者 effective ACL／無匹配 expert | ∅；`getfattr --path P --field F` 不提供有文件契約的群組/繼承/deny 算法 | 不得先讀被拒內容 | 不適用；User 核准不補 ACL adapter | UNSUPPORTED | 沒有所需角色 ACL resolver／前置檢索授權 operation。**是，核心缺口** |\\r\\n| S3 | 僅在 S2 成功後找候選／file-agent | `ug-filesystem find --path S --type file` | read | none；S2 是前置，不可跳過 | SUPPORTED | find 存在，不代表它接受目的接收者身份。否；本題上游被阻斷 |\\r\\n| S4 | 疑似敏感文字／圖像審查／document-agent、photo-agent | `ug-document extract --query Q --file P --schema auto`；`ug-photo vision-ocr --path P`；`vision-vlm --path P --prompt Q` | read | none；只讀已授權候選 | PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED | 可找線索，不是完整 PII/NER/DLP 引擎或零漏檢保證。否 |\\r\\n| S5 | 拒絕案例在檢索／snippet 前不可見／無匹配 expert | ∅；文件 qa evidence filter 未提供接收者 ACL 契約 | 要求禁止未授權 read | 不適用 | UNSUPPORTED | 不能先讀後在 prompt 隱藏，檔名/片段亦可能洩漏。**是，核心缺口** |\\r\\n| S6 | 呈現最小集合與排除理由／document-agent | `ug-document qa --query Q --file P --intent comparison --answer-mode table` | read | none；User 逐項審查 | PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED | 可草擬審查表，不能證實「最小」或「一定安全」。否 |\\r\\n| S7 | 建獨立新輸出目錄／file-agent | `ug-filesystem mkdir --path T` | state-change | gateway；核准目的地 | SUPPORTED | 普通建立目錄，不是配置接收者 ACL。否 |\\r\\n| S8 | 複製核准檔／file-agent | `ug-filesystem cpmv --src P --dst T --type copy --overwrite false` | state-change | gateway；逐項核准 | SUPPORTED | 複製本身可用；來源/輸出 ACL 是否繼承未明。否 |\\r\\n| S9 | 寫範圍、hash 與審查記錄／file-agent、knowledge-agent | `ug-filesystem sha256sum --path P`；`ug-knowledge save-text --path T --content CONTENT --filename-hint N` | read + state-change | save 為 workflow | SUPPORTED | 保存證據，不能當安全認證。否 |\\r\\n| S10 | 確保輸出只對接收者可見／無匹配 expert | ∅；`share-mkdir` 的 hide/recycle 參數不是 principal ACL 設定 | 所需權限管理 | 不適用 | UNSUPPORTED | 無角色 ACL 設定／驗證 operation；不開真實分享連結也不能忽略本地輸出越權。**是，核心缺口** |\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Official Track**：Automate Your Workflow。\\r\\n- **AI NAS Fit**：高；角色、跨資料夾、原片與輸出範圍確實依賴 NAS，但核心安全能力缺失。\\r\\n- **Capability coverage**：4/10 = **40.0%**（S4/P2/U1/X3）。下游 action 可用不等於本題可安全執行。\\r\\n- **UNKNOWN critical dependencies：5**：S1 principal/filter 語意；S4 格式/敏感片段召回；多格式來源與版本識別；S8 copy 的 ACL/metadata 行為；本地模型路由。確定性 ACL adapter、pre-snippet enforcement、輸出權限管理已列不支援，不再算未知。\\r\\n- **HARD_BLOCKER：Yes — S2/S5/S10**。\\r\\n- **Round 1 paper feasibility**：原安全承諾不能只用「最小權限」文字帶過；不能把合成 ACL fixtures 當官方 ACL API。\\r\\n- **Round 2 one-day real-device feasibility**：原流程不可保證；不能為錄 demo 先讓所有角色看同一份來源。\\r\\n- **Demo feasibility**：原題不可；手選 allowlist 的內容審查 demo 是另一個範圍，不能證明角色拒絕不洩漏。\\r\\n- **Recommended scope reduction**：另提「目前使用者已授權單一目錄的分享前內容審查清單」，明說不做接收者授權/DLP/權限保證；核心差異已變，待 User 決定。\\r\\n- **Confidence**：對公開 catalog 無此角色 ACL 契約高；不是斷言 UGOS 平台本身無 ACL。\\r\\n\\r\\n## 10. ArchiveRescue（I08；依 User 的 Top 8 順序）\\r\\n\\r\\n**原核心（M）**：只讀掛載兩個匯入來源、hash/metadata 清冊、提議同族與缺件、User 確認後產生匯入計畫及 sidecar，保留來源且可續跑。本判定嚴格保留原文的「只讀掛載」，不把它偷偷換成事先準備好的 NAS 資料夾。\\r\\n\\r\\n| Step | Workflow／expert | CLI／exact action（C） | R/W | Approval | 分類 | Known／unknown；需要不存在的能力？ |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| A1 | 將兩個外部來源以只讀掛載交給 Skill／無匹配 expert | ∅；`ug-filesystem category`／`file-workflow get-user-dir` 只能查已提供的來源 | 所需 mount/裝置配置 | 核准不能產生 mount operation | UNSUPPORTED | 無 external-device attach／read-only mount action；只呼叫 read CLI 不等同作業系統只讀掛載。**是，按原文核心步驟** |\\r\\n| A2 | 對已可讀來源做清冊／file-agent | `ug-filesystem ls --path S --recursive true --limit N`；`find --path S --type file` | read | none；真實來源已就緒才可做 | SUPPORTED | 兩來源要分開留 source root，不混路徑。否 |\\r\\n| A3 | 確認完全相同副本／file-agent | `ug-filesystem sha256sum --path P`；`ug-filemgr scan-dedupe --path S --scope all` | read | none | SUPPORTED | exact 判定以實際 SHA-256 為準；scan-dedupe 分群演算法與輸出未明。否 |\\r\\n| A4 | 保存媒體／來源 metadata／file-agent | `ug-filesystem stat --path P`；`ug-filemgr mediainfo --path P` | read | none | SUPPORTED | metadata 不證明原片真偽；不能從尺寸較大就斷定 original。否 |\\r\\n| A5 | 提議原片／壓縮衍生／同族關係／photo-agent、document-agent | `ug-photo vision-vlm --path P --prompt Q`；`ug-document qa --query Q --file P --intent comparison` | read | none | PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED | 單圖/文字線索存在；沒有暴露 perceptual hash/embedding 建庫或全格式衍生關係檢測。只能提議。否（提議型目標） |\\r\\n| A6 | 按明確 manifest 標缺件／file-agent、document-agent | `ug-filesystem cat --path M`；`ug-document reconcile --query Q --file P` | read | none；User 提供真值模板 | PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED | 依存在的清單比；不能發現毫無線索的遺失檔，不能讀損毀磁碟。否 |\\r\\n| A7 | 核准後存計畫／sidecar／file-agent | `ug-filesystem echo --path T --content CONTENT --write-mode overwrite` | state-change；只寫新輸出 | gateway；核准來源/目的地/內容 | SUPPORTED | sidecar 是資料結果，不是 executable；不動來源。否 |\\r\\n| A8 | 中斷後精確續跑且不漏檔／file-agent、knowledge-agent | `cat --path M`；`find/stat/sha256sum`；`save-text` 可作 checkpoint 輸入 | read + state-change | save 為 workflow | UNKNOWN | 大清冊分頁、穩定 fileId、來源異動與 native workflow resume/exactly-once 無契約；不宣稱已有 job engine。未證實 |\\r\\n| A9 | 保存來源與 User 決策證據／knowledge-agent | Recommended Evaluation Criteria for Future Ideas\\r\\n\\r\\n建議 Codex 選題時分兩階段：先過 **Gate**（任一不過就淘汰），再依 **官方四項等權重** 加 **風險修正** 評分。最終題目由 User 決定（`CLAUDE.md` §2、§10，DEC-0002）。\\r\\n\\r\\n### 9.1 Gates（pass/fail）\\r\\n- **G1 能力對應**：workflow 每一步都對應 catalog v2.0.0 的具體 `cli action → agent`，不依賴排程、網路、程式碼、編輯或轉檔。\\r\\n- **G2 主題契合**：能用一句話說明它是一個 AI NAS 解法，並對應一個賽道（Rules 的 pass/fail 門檻）。\\r\\n- **G3 非 wrapper**：拿掉 NAS 之後，結果明顯變差或無法產生。\\r\\n- **G4 非重疊**：不只是重新包裝某一項內建 Uliya 功能（S8）。**另須由 User 手動瀏覽 SkillHub（https://skills.ugreen.com/）確認沒有官方同名 Skill**；目前無法自動檢查。\\r\\n- **G5 可展示**：能在一個測試日內備妥非個資的展示資料，並錄成 ≤3 分鐘英文影片。\\r\\n- **G6 合規**：沒有未揭露的資料傳輸，展示與套件中不含個資或憑證。\\r\\n\\r\\n### 9.2 評分（每項 1–5；官方四項等權重）\\r\\n| 項目 | 評估問題 |\\r\\n|---|---|\\r\\n| User Value | 是否鎖定具體使用者與具體痛點？有沒有可量化的前後對比（例如時間、錯誤數、找回率）？輸出是否是「結果」而不是「建議」？ |\\r\\n| Innovation & Differentiation | ⑤ 能否用一句話說出與內建功能／一般 AI／SkillHub 的**實質**差異？撞題風險在 §5 屬於哪一級？ |\\r\\n| Technical Quality | 五種 delegation 狀態是否都處理？破壞性操作是否有 dry-run 與確認？是否明列不支援的步驟？2 核心＋1 邊界測試是否具體可執行？ |\\r\\n| AI NAS Fit & Validation | 是否使用既有資料規模、本地隱私、跨資料類型 join、或在 NAS 上留下可重用的產出？ |\\r\\n\\r\\n### 9.3 風險修正（扣分項）\\r\\n- 依賴的未驗證 action 數量（R1、R4）：每多一個關鍵假設就扣分。\\r\\n- 需要大量 gateway 核准的批次寫入（R5）。\\r\\n- 依賴本地模型推理品質（R6）。\\r\\n- 展示資料取得困難，或必須用到個資（R7）。\\r\\n- Round 1 承諾超過一個測試日能驗證的範圍（R2、R9）。\\r\\n\\r\\n### 9.4 建議提交前檢查（非開發，僅流程建議）\\r\\n- 用官方 `uliya-skill-kit` 的 `check` 跑到 `static-pass`。\\r\\n- PDF 十題逐題對應、總字數 ≤500，並明確回答 ⑤ 與 ⑨。\\r\\n- 在 ⑩ 中誠實列出 R1、R5、R6 類風險。Rules 明言缺 NAS 證據不會單獨降分（S2），誠實不會被扣分。\\r\\n\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n## 10. https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/winning-entries-gemma-4-good-challenge/ ; https://openai.devpost.com/project-gallery ; https://shop.zimaspace.com/pages/show-us-your-zimaos | Google / OpenAI / Devpost / ZimaSpace | **非 UGREEN 證據**，只用於模式推論，可轉移性中低。另：GitHub、Reddit、YouTube 搜尋未發現本賽公開作品 |\\r\\n\\r\\n**研究限制**：Devpost 頁面對 WebFetch 回 403（Cloudflare），改以研究代理用 curl 下載的公開頁面本機副本為準；Claude 已逐段閱讀 Rules 與 Overview 原文。沒有登入、沒有繞過驗證、沒有提交任何內容。\\r\\n# Top 8：UGREEN 官方 capability 技術可行性 mapping\\r\\n\\r\\nTask：**TOP8-TECH-FEASIBILITY-001**。日期：2026-11-01，Asia/Taipei。\\r\\n本文件不做市場評分、不選最終題目、不建立 Skill 或產品。AUTO-007 保持 **PAUSED_BY_USER**。\\r\\n\\r\\n## 1. 輸入、證據版本與判定方式\\r\\n\\r\\n- **I：競賽情報**：[docs/09_COMPETITION_INTELLIGENCE.md](09_COMPETITION_INTELLIGENCE.md)，正式 commit `41d7bc0`。\\r\\n- **M：候選原定義**：[市場研究 §6](https://github.com/DevonForlan/ugreen-ai-nas-hackathon-2026/blob/f3567222ed9303cac02351af35d54984be014f0f/docs/MARKET_IDEATION_RESEARCH_2026-11-01.md)。開始時尚未同步，查核期間已正式發布；本報告固定使用 **f3567222ed9303cac02351af35d54984be014f0f**，不是未提交草稿，也不沿用其市場分數。\\r\\n- **C：官方機器可讀能力目錄**：[UGREEN kit 0.7.3 官方 ZIP](https://cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0630/9416/1602/files/uliya-skill-kit-0.7.3-en.zip) 的 `data/capability-catalog.json`；catalog **2.0.0**、generatedAt **2026-09-01**；9 CLI／6 experts／78 actions。下載 ZIP SHA-256：`cf557e5a022e4297892f39768132855154232efdedeb726b2845450ccd70e1b3`。只讀取文件/JSON，未執行套件或產品程式。\\r\\n- **D：開發標準**：同 ZIP 的 `docs/skill-development-standard.md`、`references/cli-capabilities.md`。catalog 是 action、參數、effect、approval 的優先來源；找不到所需 operation 就列不支援，不從產品名稱推導。\\r\\n- **R：[官方 Rules](https://ugreenhackathon.devpost.com/rules)**，What to Create／Special Notes：只能透過已註冊 expert 使用支援的 CLI；不能以自訂 executable、通用 runtime、未授權直接存取補洞。Round 1 無須 NAS runtime 證據；Round 2 必須在指定 NAS 實際執行，僅一個指定測試日。\\r\\n- **H：[官方 Starter Kit 頁](https://ai.ugreen.com/pages/hackathon-starter-kit)** §5–7：先確認 scope、使用前一步真實結果；移動、覆寫、刪除、對外分享先確認；完成與失敗不能捏造。\\r\\n\\r\\n**重要修正**：I 的高階摘要不能取代 C。C 有 `ug-filesystem restore --path`，範例使用 `#recycle` 路徑；這是回收項目還原的線索，**不是備份系統／snapshot restore API 的證據**。C 也明列 `sha256sum`、`echo`、`touch`，所以不能說「沒有 hash 或沒有寫檔」。\\r\\n\\r\\n### 四種分類\\r\\n\\r\\n| 分類 | 本報告的精確意思 |\\r\\n|---|---|\\r\\n| SUPPORTED | C 明列可完成該步基本效果的 action／參數；只代表文件對應，不代表裝置已驗證 |\\r\\n| PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED | 部分基本 operation 存在，但原步驟包含品質、組合、格式、範圍或語意保證，catalog 不足以完整證實 |\\r\\n| UNKNOWN | 有可能依靠現有 action／平台，但缺必要的輸出、語意或裝置條件，尚不能判定可做 |\\r\\n| UNSUPPORTED | 在本版公開 Skill capability 中沒有匹配 operation，且不能合法用自訂程式補足；不是宣稱整個 UGOS 產品永遠沒有此功能 |\\r\\n\\r\\n**HARD_BLOCKER**：原候選核心流程有不可省略的 UNSUPPORTED 步驟即為 Yes。不能以人工預先加工、影片回放、改成建議清單、改成手動掃描，暗中把原題改成可行。下面的 scope reduction 都是**另列的提案，尚未獲 User 核准，不改變原題判定**。\\r\\n\\r\\n**Capability coverage % = SUPPORTED 步驟數 ÷ 該題表內步驟總數 × 100**，四捨五入一位小數；PARTIAL／UNKNOWN／UNSUPPORTED 都不計已確認覆蓋。每列是一個可觀察結果，多 action 的列取較保守分類。人工確認寫在 approval 欄，不另加一個容易的 SUPPORTED 步驟灌水。這是文件覆蓋率，**不是成功率、完整度、創新分數或市場分數**；高覆蓋率不能抵銷 HARD_BLOCKER。\\r\\n\\r\\n**UNKNOWN critical dependencies**：各題 U 清單中互相獨立、會阻止原承諾被驗證的問題數；也包含 PARTIAL 列中未證實的關鍵條件，不等於 UNKNOWN 列數。已判 UNSUPPORTED 的需求不再重複算 UNKNOWN。\\r\\n\\r\\n## 2. 呼叫、批准與跨題共同邊界\\r\\n\\r\\n主 Skill 只做編排／提問；操作交給表內 expert，例如 `delegate --target file-agent --instruction '<限定工作>'`。表內簡記 `CLI action --option value` 的**正式呼叫**均為：\\r\\n\\r\\n```text\\r\\ncli.invoke --cli <CLI> --action <ACTION> -- <catalog-defined options>\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n這些是能力對應記法，**本次沒有執行**。`P/S/M/T` 分別是實際回傳或使用者核准的檔案路徑／來源目錄／manifest／新輸出路徑，`Q` 是明確查詢，`ID` 是真實回傳 ID；不能猜路徑，不能把 photo UUID、subtitle file-id 與 filesystem fileId 互換。參數值、JSON/CSV 序列化及跨 expert ID 對應仍須實機確認。\\r\\n\\r\\n- `approval=none` 只表示沒有明列操作確認，不代表有讀取所有資料的權利。C 的 action permissions、prerequisites、errorCodes、stability 均缺完整契約；**不能把 scope 提示詞當 ACL 隔離**。\\r\\n- `gateway`、`workflow`、`conditional` 保留 C 原值。寫入前還須呈現真實清單、目的路徑、覆寫風險與 User 核准；不能拿一次口頭同意冒充所有 gateway prompt 已通過。\\r\\n- 只接受 `completed` 的真實結果；`waiting_user`／`waiting_approval` 不算完成，`failed`／`cancelled` 停止或回報部分結果。CLI envelope `ok=true` 不等於內容正確；核對回傳 coverage、failures、counts、路徑／ID。\\r\\n- `file-workflow batch-move`、`batch-del`、`run` 有批次 action，不代表一次核准、原子交易、自動 rollback 或 exactly-once；`total/succeeded/failed/hasMore` 要逐項追蹤。\\r\\n- 原市場研究假設可用 Docker/Python、自建 embedding、背景 ASR。這些**不是本賽 Skill 的部署選項**。可用官方 expert 取得相同結果時才 mapping；不能把 research stack 當作可隨包上傳的依賴。\\r\\n- 本地推論／不外傳必須驗證實際平台模型路由；「用了 NAS」不是零外傳證據。本文件沒有登入裝置、執行模型或取得實機結果。\\r\\n\\r\\n## 3. 必查能力逐項結論\\r\\n\\r\\n| 能力 | Expert／精確 CLI action | R/W、approval（C） | 支援範圍與限制 |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| 備份／還原 | file-agent：`ug-filesystem cpmv --src P --dst T --type copy --overwrite false`；`restore --path P` | state-change，gateway | 複製既有目錄式副本可 mapping；restore 範例是回收路徑。建立備份 job、選 backup snapshot、應用 DB 一致性復原：UNSUPPORTED；restore 衝突／範圍：UNKNOWN |\\r\\n| Metadata／hash | file-agent：`ug-filesystem stat --path P`、`getfattr --path P --field F`、`sha256sum --path P`、`md5sum --path P` | read，none | 基本查詢／hash SUPPORTED；stat 欄位、xattr 命名、掃描一致性 UNKNOWN。MD5 不用作安全完整性證明；metadata 能讀不代表全檔可解碼 |\\r\\n| ACL／permission awareness | file-agent：`ug-filesystem category --permission V`；photo-agent：`ug-photo albums --album-perm V` | read，none | 可傳 permission filter，但 V 語意不明，PARTIAL。沒有任意接收者 effective ACL／群組繼承／deny 交集／分享連結權限契約；不可當作 ShareSafe 的 ACL adapter |\\r\\n| Audio transcription | knowledge-agent：`ug-knowledge audio-transcription --path P --language L --speak-count N` | read，none | action／transcriptText／transcriptRef／segments 有列；WAV/其他 codec、混語、speaker 標記及 segment 時間精度 UNKNOWN；不能假定接受影片或自動抽音 |\\r\\n| Photo search／VLM／OCR | photo-agent：`ug-photo search-picture`、`search-screen --category custom --keyword Q`、`meta --path P`、`vision-ocr --path P`、`vision-vlm --path P --prompt Q` | read，none | metadata/人/時間/OCR 篩選與單圖 VLM 存在。search-picture 沒有一般 `--query` 參數；不能從宣傳「semantic search」推導任意文字→影格 embedding API、品質或自行建索引能力 |\\r\\n| Video search／字幕／metadata | media-agent：`ug-video search --type query --keyword Q`、`subtitle-search --video-path P --query Q`、`subtitle-download --video-path P --file-id ID`；file-agent：`ug-filemgr mediainfo --path P` | search=mixed/none（不傳 play）；字幕搜尋=read/none；下載=state-change/gateway；mediainfo=read/none | 內容庫搜尋、字幕來源與媒體資訊有列；無影格抽樣、影片 ASR/embedding 建索引、時間碼對齊、生成預覽／剪輯 action。字幕下載只用真實搜尋 ID，不是從原片產生字幕 |\\r\\n| 文件 extraction／reconcile | document-agent：`ug-document extract --query Q --file P --schema auto`、`qa --query Q --file P --intent comparison --content-scope full`、`reconcile --query Q --file P` | read，none | 有抽取／比較／對帳，但 PDF/DOCX/CSV/JPEG 的支援集合、分頁與證據定位精度 UNKNOWN；缺件提示不能升格為履約／法律認證 |\\r\\n| Knowledge save／evidence | knowledge-agent：`ug-knowledge collect-evidence --query Q --path S --full true`、`save-text --path T --content-ref REF --filename-hint N` | evidence=read/none；save=state-change/workflow | 核對 `verified=true/outputFile/contentBytes`；這是寫入證據，不是來源事實、ACL、永久索引或 append-only 歷史的證明 |\\r\\n| Event trigger／scheduler | 無已註冊 expert/action 可執行 | 不適用 | UNSUPPORTED。surveillance event-timeline 是讀取既有監控事件，不是檔案事件訂閱；音樂 stop timer 不是工作排程器 |\\r\\n| Notification | 無已註冊 expert/action 可寄 Email/IM、OS push 或背景通知 | 不適用 | UNSUPPORTED；Skill 回覆／存文字供人查看，不等同主動提醒 |\\r\\n| File change detection | file-agent：手動再次 `find/stat/sha256sum`，與已存 manifest 比較 | read/none；保存新 baseline 需寫入 approval | 使用者觸發的兩次比較 PARTIAL；檔案事件 watch、可靠刪除/改名 event stream、背景增量 reindex 無 action |\\r\\n| Batch write | file-agent：`file-workflow start --kind organize --target S --destination T` → `run --id ID`；`batch-move --data DATA`、`batch-del --paths PATHS` | start=read/none；其餘 state-change/workflow | 批次操作存在；DATA/PATHS 格式、可批次批准範圍、錯誤補償與續跑 UNKNOWN。不要用 batch-move 冒充 batch-copy |\\r\\n| 一般結果檔 | file-agent：`ug-filesystem echo --path T --content CONTENT --write-mode overwrite`；knowledge-agent：`save-text` | state-change，gateway／workflow | 可存 JSON/CSV/Markdown **資料結果**；不因此取得執行 JS/Python、部署 web app、影片生成、ACL 設定能力。覆寫只對核准新輸出檔 |\\r\\n| 相簿／時間軸 | photo-agent：`ug-photo create-album --album-name N --add-item IDS`、`timeline --group-by month` | state-change，workflow | 即使叫 timeline 也不是無副作用唯讀查詢。照片時間與故事順序可先從 meta/ASR 組成文字，不偷跑有副作用的相簿整理 |\\r\\n\\r\\n## 4. RestoreLens（I01）\\r\\n\\r\\n**原核心（M）**：從一種既有目錄式備份找出資產與相依項，核准後還原到新目錄，驗證 hash、可解碼及引用關係。M 明說不碰真實還原 API；因此不會因沒有 backup job API 就把它判死，但也不能刪掉可解碼驗證來過關。\\r\\n\\r\\n| Step | Workflow／expert | CLI／exact action（C） | R/W | Approval | 分類 | Known／unknown；需要不存在的能力？ |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| R1 | 取得核准來源清冊／file-agent | `file-workflow get-user-dir`；`ug-filesystem ls --path S --recursive true --limit N` | read | none；User 指定 scope | SUPPORTED | 真實目錄／fileId；完整列舉與 limit 截斷未知。否 |\\r\\n| R2 | 將資產需求連到 manifest 相依項／file-agent、document-agent | `ug-filesystem cat --path M`；`ug-document qa --query Q --file P --intent extraction` | read | none | PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED | 能讀文字與擷取；不能保證任意 app DB/相依圖完整。無專用 dependency engine |\\r\\n| R3 | 查找兩份既有備份目錄與候選／file-agent | `ug-filesystem find --path S --type file`；`stat --path P` | read | none | SUPPORTED | 查已存在副本；不是建立備份或 snapshot restore。否 |\\r\\n| R4 | 缺件清單、來源與目的地預覽／file-agent | `cat --path M`；`sha256sum --path P`，將真實結果交回主 Skill 核對 | read | none；等待 User 確認 | PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED | 指定 manifest 可比對；未列入的依賴無法憑模型找全。否，但完整性保證未建立 |\\r\\n| R5 | 複製到隔離新目錄／file-agent | `ug-filesystem mkdir --path T`；`cpmv --src P --dst T --type copy --overwrite false` | state-change | gateway；逐項目的地確認 | SUPPORTED | 目錄式復原副本；copy 屬性／ACL 與非原子中斷行為未知。否 |\\r\\n| R6 | 對來源與輸出逐檔 hash／file-agent | `ug-filesystem sha256sum --path P`；`stat --path P` | read | none | SUPPORTED | 精確記錄兩側 hash；不使用 LLM 產生 hash。相同 hash 不證明應用可開啟。否 |\\r\\n| R7 | 確定性全媒體可解碼驗證／無匹配 expert | ∅；`ug-filemgr mediainfo --path P` 只是近似候選，不提供完整 decode 驗證 | 需求為 read；無可用 operation | 核准無法補能力 | UNSUPPORTED | 無完整解碼／壞幀／截斷媒體驗證 action；播放成功或 metadata 存在不等價。**是，核心缺口** |\\r\\n| R8 | 還原引用是否可解析且應用可重用／file-agent、photo-agent | `cat --path M`／`ug-photo meta --path P` 只供部分核對 | read | none | UNKNOWN | 普通 manifest 邊可查；相簿/app 的 ID、路徑重綁與 import/驗證契約未明。未證實 |\\r\\n| R9 | 保存有範圍與失敗項的報告／knowledge-agent | `ug-knowledge save-text --path T --content CONTENT --filename-hint N` | state-change | workflow；核准內容 | SUPPORTED | 要真實 outputFile/verified，不把「已寫報告」當「已成功災復」。否 |\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Official Track**：Open Innovation（技術對應，非最終賽道核准）。\\r\\n- **AI NAS Fit**：高；使用原位長期副本、來源相依與可保存驗證證據，非單檔摘要。\\r\\n- **Capability coverage**：5/9 = **55.6%**（S5/P2/U1/X1）。\\r\\n- **UNKNOWN critical dependencies：6**：R1 全量列舉；R2 manifest/依賴完整性；R5 copy/metadata/中斷語意；R8 ID/引用重綁；來源與目的地實際授權；本地模型路由。R7 是已知不支援，不另算未知。\\r\\n- **HARD_BLOCKER：Yes — R7**。\\r\\n- **Round 1 paper feasibility**：原承諾不可誠實宣稱完整可行；必須揭露 R7，不可因 Round 1 無 NAS 就略過。\\r\\n- **Round 2 one-day real-device feasibility**：原流程不可完整驗證；即使 copy/hash 可跑也不能交付解碼證明。\\r\\n- **Demo feasibility**：可畫流程／展示子流程；不能以事先用外部工具驗碼冒充 Skill 完成 R7。\\r\\n- **Recommended scope reduction**：另提「目錄副本＋指定 manifest＋SHA-256 證據」，明確移除完整解碼與 app 可用性保證；**這是改題範圍，待 User 決定**。\\r\\n- **Confidence**：對 R7 catalog 缺口高；對 copy/引用實機行為低。\\r\\n\\r\\n## 5. CreatorReady（I02）\\r\\n\\r\\n**原核心（M）**：原片低頻影格＋ASR，依 brief 找片段，時間碼連回原片，核對授權與代理檔，輸出 manifest 和小型預覽；不是單純影片標題搜尋。\\r\\n\\r\\n| Step | Workflow／expert | CLI／exact action（C） | R/W | Approval | 分類 | Known／unknown；需要不存在的能力？ |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| C1 | 專案素材清冊／file-agent | `ug-filesystem find --path S --type video`；`ug-filemgr mediainfo --path P` | read | none；User 核准範圍 | SUPPORTED | 路徑與媒體資訊；codec 欄位／來源全量性待測。否 |\\r\\n| C2 | 從原片抽低頻影格／無匹配 expert | ∅ | 所需 read + 產生衍生影格 | 不適用 | UNSUPPORTED | 無 frame extraction/影片處理 action；不得附 ffmpeg/Python 補足。**是，核心缺口** |\\r\\n| C3 | 原片語音→帶時間的轉錄／knowledge-agent | `ug-knowledge audio-transcription --path P` | read（宣告值） | none | UNKNOWN | 音檔 ASR 有列，但是否接受影片容器／可無損抽音未明；不能假設支援。未證實 |\\r\\n| C4 | 影格＋語音建立可查片段索引／無匹配 expert | ∅；media-agent 的 `ug-video search --type query --keyword Q` 不等於建立 shot index | 無索引建置 operation | 不適用 | UNSUPPORTED | 無自建 embedding/shot index/檢索服務 action。**是，核心缺口** |\\r\\n| C5 | 候選連回正確原片時間碼／media-agent、knowledge-agent | `ug-video search`、ASR `segments` 僅為可能輸入 | read/mixed；不傳 play | none | UNKNOWN | 無明列 video seek/timecoded-shot contract；control 只有 pause/continue/next/previous。未證實 |\\r\\n| C6 | 查授權 CSV 與代理檔是否齊／file-agent、document-agent | `ug-filesystem cat --path M`；`find --path S --pattern PATTERN`；`ug-document reconcile --query Q --file P` | [{"type": "input_text", "text": "Script completed\nWall time 4.5 seconds\nOutput:\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "{\"chunk_id\":\"0e390d\",\"wall_time_seconds\":1.1110606,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":12967,\"output\":\"# Vertical Workflow Discovery — 2026-11-01\\r\\n\\r\\nTask: VERTICAL-WORKFLOW-DISCOVERY-001. Research only; no topic selected, product, Skill, UI or demo created. Access date for public research: 2026-11-01 (Asia/Taipei). Assigned task commit: `6c2ef3325ad7eb8a3af81385ba889f393a8be8c5`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## A. Executive conclusion\\r\\n\\r\\n**INFERENCE:** The stronger opportunity is a bounded professional **handoff review**, after search has already found the files: reconcile the actual asset/sample/interview identity, competing document versions, missing evidence and required human disposition. “RAG with citations” is insufficient differentiation. Public evidence supports these jobs, but does **not** yet establish an underserved market or a winning entry.\\r\\n\\r\\nExactly six directions merit further review: **SourceWeave, ConditionReturn, ConsentBoundary, CaseBinder—TradeCloseout, SampleTrail and ServiceEvidence**. Three particularly interesting next investigations are **ConditionReturn, SampleTrail and SourceWeave**, without selecting a winner. ConditionReturn and SampleTrail provide more specific professional review problems than family memory; neither has demonstrated superior customer demand, NAS deployment or competitive advantage. Their mature vertical competitors are substantial counterevidence.\\r\\n\\r\\n- **MemoryWeave: not retained.** FACT: Remento and Storyworth already address family voice/story/photo preservation [S51–S52]. INFERENCE: provenance and disagreement labels alone do not overcome HIGH generic-AI collision; NAS necessity and costly consumer demand are weakly evidenced.\\r\\n- **CaseBinder: conditional survival only as TradeCloseout.** FACT: Procore and Pype already address closeout requirements and packages [S25–S27]. INFERENCE: installed-equipment-ID versus supplier-document discrepancies across local exports are a testable narrower hypothesis, not a novel generic binder. Collision remains HIGH.\\r\\n- **SourceWeave: conditionally supported.** FACT: documentary checking has a real assertion→primary-source→page/quote workflow [S09]; archival production has a distinct sourcing/clearance role [S10]. INFERENCE: cross-source contradictions before editorial approval are a stronger job than asset search. Trint and on-premises Axle already solve much of the surrounding workflow [S11–S12]. A gap is not yet proven.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Evidence and inference discipline\\r\\n\\r\\n**FACT** denotes what a cited source actually documents, including vendor descriptions rather than measured performance. **INFERENCE** denotes this research's interpretation or proposed design. **ASSUMPTION** is a condition needing customer/device validation. **UNKNOWN** is unverified. All scores, collision ratings, costs and proposed workflows are INFERENCE unless explicitly marked otherwise. Qualitative costs are plausible consequences, not observed incidence or invented ROI. No interviews, paid-product trials, competing-model runs, NAS experiments or customer willingness-to-pay measurements were conducted.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe workflow job and records were researched before product framing. Names below are handles assigned to the identified jobs; a special industry/name is not novelty. Historical practitioner guidance is dated in the log and used as workflow evidence, not proof of a current unmet market. Current served product documentation supplies competitive counterevidence. No source absence is treated as product absence.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Existing context and official constraints\\r\\n\\r\\nRead in full: [competition intelligence](09_COMPETITION_INTELLIGENCE.md), [market research](MARKET_IDEATION_RESEARCH_2026-11-01.md), [critical review](TOP8_CRITICAL_REVIEW_2026-11-01.md), [technical feasibility](TOP8_TECH_FEASIBILITY_2026-11-01.md) and [project status](00_PROJECT_STATUS.md). These are P01–P05 respectively. This document **does not redo** the Top 8 capability mapping. Official rules [S01] and official kit/catalog constraints take precedence over earlier ideas.\\r\\n\\r\\n**FACT:** The rules use four equally weighted criteria: User Value, Innovation & Differentiation, Technical Quality, AI NAS Fit & Validation. Round 1 requires the ten-question proposal totaling at most 500 English words, PDF and initial Skill ZIP; Round 2 retains the same project, has one designated device-test day and a public English video under three minutes [S01]. The <=500-word passages below are short explanations, **not** completed submissions or Skills. No Round 1 device proof is assumed mandatory. Current starter-kit landing page [S02] was checked, but its rendered text did not expose a new version; capability conclusions inherit P04's pinned kit 0.7.3/catalog 2.0.0 audit, not a claim to have validated a newer runtime.\\r\\n\\r\\n**FACT (catalog-bounded, P04):** The public surface is Markdown orchestration over registered experts/actions. Document extraction/QA/comparison/reconciliation, single-photo OCR/VLM/metadata, audio transcription, evidence collection and saving text may support bounded review. Arbitrary Python/Docker/backend, generic HTTP, scheduler/file-event triggers, notifications, backup/snapshot APIs, effective ACL enforcement, video frame extraction/edit/render and executable custom scripts are unavailable. A path, photo UUID and subtitle ID are not interchangeable. Do not assume one QA action accepts arbitrary multi-file batches: read selected files individually, then reconcile returned outputs in the main context. Unknown throughput must not become a promised pipeline.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Three competitive layers and the actual substitution test\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **NAS/local layer — FACT:** UGREEN already markets universal/file/knowledge/photo search, OCR and voice features [S03]. Qsirch markets AI search/RAG over local content [S04]. Current Synology Photos documentation covers photo management/search/sharing [S05]; no claim is made that an unobserved feature is absent. Axle explicitly supports existing NAS/SAN and on-premises media AI [S12]. Datashare and OMERO show specialized self-hosted/local alternatives [S40,S44]. SkillHub was not readable during research: overlap there is **UNKNOWN**, not “clear.” Earlier Top 8 research covers Immich/Paperless/Nextcloud; those generic capabilities are not repeated as supposed new gaps.\\r\\n2. **Vertical layer — FACT:** Articheck/EMu, OHMS/Aviary, Procore/Pype, ESdat/EQuIS and Beamex/Fluke already complete substantial portions of the shortlisted jobs [S18–S19,S23–S27,S31–S38]. Proposed remaining gaps are specifically marked ASSUMPTION, not claims of missing competitor features. OHMS and Aviary are two integrated adjacent tools, not independent vendors.\\r\\n3. **General AI layer — FACT:** ChatGPT projects support files/context and documented local-folder projects; Claude projects support project knowledge; Gemini accepts multimodal files [S06–S08]. **INFERENCE:** Given the same authorized small files and checklist, capable general AI can reproduce much of the reasoning. “Cloud cannot read/cite files” is false. Local RAG likewise supplies retrieval and citations. NAS value must survive a fair same-input comparison: persistent originals, scope coverage, repeatable version/identity linkage, reliable source locations, human-reviewed state and saved handoff artifact. If these do not reduce actual work, reject the idea.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Shared conditional scope, storage evidence and device gates\\r\\n\\r\\n**INFERENCE:** All retained concepts are manually launched, bounded, evidence-linked **prechecks**. They produce `supported / contradicted / missing evidence / unreadable / unknown`, never `true / compliant / approved`. Missing means absent from an explicitly enumerated selection, not absent from the world. User supplies an authoritative checklist and version-precedence rule. A person approves conclusions and any new saved report; originals stay unchanged. No notification, external submission, signatures, access changes or automatic publishing is needed. Returned source pointers must be genuine; a hash is byte identity, not authenticity or an immutable chain of custody. Source text cannot authorize tool actions.\\r\\n\\r\\nStorage grading: **N3** = an actual NAS case in the relevant/adjacent sector; **N2** = documented local repository/shared-server/LAN workflow; **N1** = plausible local files only. N3 adjacent evidence is not direct proof for a specific job. An institutional film archive is neither an SMB sample nor proof that condition reports use NAS. Target users must **already** retain the relevant originals on shared/local storage; migration itself is not a proven benefit. Privacy is an incentive, not a guarantee: **UNKNOWN** whether every expert/model route remains local or transmits content. No zero-exfiltration claim.\\r\\n\\r\\nFor explicit unknown counts, independent dependency groups are: **G1** supported input formats/extraction coverage; **G2** stable record IDs and real page/segment/image source locations; **G3** cross-source reasoning accuracy and abstention; **G4** bounded enumeration/context/latency; **G5** approved new-file save behavior; **G6** actual model/network route. Counts are research bookkeeping, not measured defect counts. Cards list additional domain groups. All six are conditional until tested; “no known core unsupported action” is not proof of runtime support.\\r\\n\\r\\n**INFERENCE — prospective device plan, not execution:** Reserve roughly six active hours within the assigned day: establish formats/source pointers first, run two small planted-error cases and one clean/ambiguous control, review failures, then capture the <3-minute explanation. Audio only where essential; typically 10–24 synthetic PDFs/images/text records and 1–2 short audio inputs. No precomputed AI answers, external OCR/ffmpeg/embeddings, arbitrary scripts or concealed services. A failed core gate kills the defined topic rather than silently shrinking it. Customer evidence, format compatibility and repeatability remain separate from an appealing synthetic demo.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Generic-AI Collision Test\\r\\n\\r\\n**INFERENCE — analytical simulation, not actual ChatGPT/Claude/Gemini calls or observed submission statistics.** Simulated prompt A: “Give me 20 innovative AI NAS hackathon ideas for photos, documents and video.” Expected recurring families: photo organizer, family memory, document Q&A, semantic file search, dedupe/cleanup, receipts/invoices, backup assistant, knowledge base, creator asset search, surveillance summary and smart file organizer. Prompt B: “Make them trustworthy with citations, conflicts and human approval.” Expected outcome: evidence binders and provenance-enhanced versions of those same families; these additions do not create a moat. Prompt C adds a profession: museum, sampling lab, archivist or metrology coordinator. A capable model can then readily suggest condition comparison, custody reconciliation, consent checks or certificate review.\\r\\n\\r\\n**INFERENCE:** No candidate earns LOW from a name or obscure sector. MEDIUM means less likely in an unseeded broad brainstorm but readily generated with domain context, with meaningful nearby competitors. HIGH means an obvious family or end-to-end incumbent overlap. Underlying jobs, not slogans, are evaluated. Actual entrant distribution and SkillHub overlap remain UNKNOWN.\\r\\n\\r\\n| Workflow | Closest obvious family | Underlying collision | What could distinguish it, if validated |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| W01 | Creator asset search / evidence binder | MEDIUM | Exact assertion-to-primary-source exceptions before editorial sign-off |\\r\\n| W02 | Private document RAG | HIGH | No proven gap over Datashare/Aleph |\\r\\n| W03 | Photo inspection / inspection report | MEDIUM | Loan/object identity and required-view reconciliation, not damage diagnosis |\\r\\n| W04 | Knowledge base / compliance assistant | MEDIUM | Pre-deposit restriction conflict, explicitly separate from ACL enforcement |\\r\\n| W05 | Document completeness / compliance binder | HIGH | Installed-ID discrepancies across unmanaged supplier exports |\\r\\n| W06 | Revision summary | HIGH | Geometry is essential but unsupported |\\r\\n| W07 | Claim/receipt extraction | HIGH | Weak NAS-native advantage |\\r\\n| W08 | Field inspection / compliance assistant | MEDIUM | Plan-to-label-to-lab alias and intentional QA duplicate handling |\\r\\n| W09 | Research assistant / integrity check | MEDIUM | Scientific transformation validation is unsupported |\\r\\n| W10 | Image classification / surveillance | HIGH | No validated specialist species/scale capability |\\r\\n| W11 | Creator asset / rights assistant | HIGH | Existing DAM/licensing overlap, gap unproven |\\r\\n| W12 | Maintenance / certificate assistant | MEDIUM | Cross-vendor identity exceptions, not calibration validity |\\r\\n| W13 | Lecture/transcript assistant | HIGH | Actual video alignment/remediation unsupported |\\r\\n| W14 | Family memory assistant | HIGH | Conflict labels alone do not justify retention |\\r\\n\\r\\n## C. Full candidate cards\\r\\n\\r\\nThe numbered fields correspond to task §5. Proposed costs, workflow designs, feasibility, objections and kill gates are INFERENCE. Competitor descriptions are FACT only to the extent supported by cited documentation. Each English explanation is below 500 words; none is a submission package.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W01 — SourceWeave: documentary factual-assertion review\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **Primary user — FACT:** Documentary archival producer / independent checker [S09–S10].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Connect selected script assertions to primary evidence and unresolved contradictions before editorial review.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Interviews, documents, archive images and claim/check records [S09–S10]. ASSUMPTION: draft assertions and rights notes are readable exports, not an NLE timeline.\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — FACT:** A film-production NAS case retains projects/production records [S13]. INFERENCE: N3 sector evidence; actual checker deployment unknown.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Practitioner uses a facts/links/page/quote/checker spreadsheet and independent checking [S09].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Search finds an asset, but does not establish whether the edited assertion is supported or contradicts another source.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Rechecking, delivery delay, corrections and reputational exposure; no quantified incidence/ROI.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Trint Story Builder and Axle AI [S11–S12]; general substitutes [S06–S08].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Transcript excerpts→narrative in Trint; catalog/transcripts/AI metadata and on-prem NAS media in Axle [S11–S12].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Cross-folder assertion→primary support→contradiction→checker disposition outside the current MAM. Not a proven missing Axle feature.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Same small authorized inputs can reproduce this. Advantage must be persistent corpus coverage, real pointers and repeatable review artifacts, not better prose or citations alone (§A).\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Review locally retained media/history with registered document/photo/audio experts; UNKNOWN actual network/model routing.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Exact assertion support, date/identity contradictions and missing primary support within enumerated scope.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Truth, sufficiency, clearance, narrative decisions and public use remain with checker/producer.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central; actual file, passage/location and contradictory evidence. Hash ≠ authenticity/custody proof.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Genuine interview/document/photo comparison; no raw-video interpretation or irrelevant photo garnish.\\r\\n17. **Capability fit — INFERENCE:** Per-file document QA/extract/reconcile, single-photo OCR/VLM/meta, audio transcription, evidence collection, saved text [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 + audio format/segment-location reliability = **7 groups**. Unsupported: raw-video extraction, NLE integration, custom index, scheduler, rights adjudication.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** SourceWeave helps documentary fact-checkers review assertions against selected interviews, archive images and documents already on a NAS. It returns support, contradictions and missing primary evidence with real source pointers for human approval. It does not edit video, certify truth or clear rights. The first device test must prove source-location fidelity and abstention.\\r\\n20. **One-day device feasibility — INFERENCE:** CONDITIONAL, 12–20 synthetic files, one short interview, two script revisions. Fail if pointers/extraction fail; no hidden preprocessing.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** 0:00–0:30 “opened in 1972”; 0:30–1:30 interview versus dated 1974 source; 1:30–2:20 actual pointers plus unsupported causal assertion; 2:20–3:00 human leaves unresolved and saves. Not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS feature collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH for search/OCR/summary, MEDIUM for full assertion-review job [S03–S05].\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** MEDIUM, with substantial Trint/Axle vertical overlap.\\r\\n24. **Generic LLM suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy after documentary context; evidence labels do not create a moat.\\r\\n25. **Judge objection — INFERENCE:** “Trint plus a prompt; Axle already runs locally.”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Documented costly post-search job and credible media-data gravity; conflict is legible to judges.\\r\\n27. **Kill gate — INFERENCE:** Practitioners already do this smoothly in their MAM, pointers are fabricated, or same-input generic AI has no meaningful workflow disadvantage.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W02 — LeadTrace: investigative prepublication dossier\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Investigative journalist [S40–S41].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Prepare allegation-to-evidence review distinguishing ambiguous identities and missing support.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Documents, scans and extracted entities/connections [S40–S41].\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — FACT:** Datashare supports self-hosted/local analysis [S40]. INFERENCE: N2, not UGREEN demand.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Datashare/Aleph extraction, search and investigation structures [S40–S41].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Same-name entities or inference chains become unsupported allegations.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Rework, editorial delay and reputational/identity harm; no legal outcome predicted.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Datashare and Aleph [S40–S41].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** OCR/extraction/search, entities and investigative relationships/timelines [S40–S41].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Final cross-export assertion ledger; no proven gap in these strong tools.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Strong same-input models can check claims; a private-RAG wrapper is insufficient.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Local history fits storage, but specialist self-hosted tools may be a better fit.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Passage support, entity ambiguity and chronology, never autonomous accusation.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Editor/reporter decides identity, truth, source protection and publication.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central; actual passages and unresolved ambiguity.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Optional for the central text dossier; do not force it.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Document QA/extract/reconcile/save; no graph service or web investigation assumed [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 = **6 groups**. Unsupported: registry enrichment, graph backend, ACL enforcement.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** LeadTrace would prepare an allegation checklist from selected local investigation records, preserving direct passages, unresolved identities and contradictions. It would neither accuse people nor publish. Datashare and Aleph already solve much of the job; a credible entry first needs evidence of an unmet cross-export review step.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** Tiny synthetic dossier feasible conditionally; no validation of newsroom adoption/confidentiality.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** Two same-name entities and unsupported allegation; abstention shown. Not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH private document RAG.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH evidence binder.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy with journalist context.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “Why not Datashare/Aleph and the editorial checklist?”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Serious error cost and established self-hosting.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Reject from Top 6 without evidence of a remaining specialist-tool handoff gap.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W03 — ConditionReturn: museum loan-return reconciliation\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Museum registrar with conservator [S15–S17].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Reconcile returned object identity, outgoing/return reports and required image views before accepting the record.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Loan/object IDs, condition notes and photographs [S15–S17].\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — FACT:** Museum NAS archive exists [S14]. INFERENCE: N3 adjacent sector, only N1 for loan records; large film archives do not prove small-museum report storage.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Review previous condition, identify records, photograph/check condition and document changes [S15–S17].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Wrong loan/object, missing view or unexplained change hides behind a complete-looking folder.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Return clarification, conservation triage and disputed responsibility; no damage/liability diagnosis.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Articheck and Axiell EMu [S18–S19].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Multimedia condition history/annotations in Articheck; conservation/condition history with multimedia in EMu [S18–S19].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Historic nonstandard exports against a current loan checklist, outside integrated collections tools.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Same-input models can compare records. Need better NAS scope/identity/source continuity, not a smarter damage description.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Existing originals and review artifact share persistent local storage; actual local custody must be established.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** IDs, dates, stated-condition disagreements and user-required view coverage; visual observation is a hypothesis.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Conservator decides damage/change; registrar approves acceptance, loan status and communications.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central; specific object, source and observation, with unresolved mappings.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Necessary for photo/report/object-view correspondence; not calibrated pixel-level comparison.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Single-photo OCR/VLM/meta, document extract/compare/reconcile, saved text [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 + object/view mapping = **7 groups**. Unsupported: annotation renderer, measured damage detection, collection-system integration or automatic claims.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** ConditionReturn helps a registrar check a returned museum loan against outgoing records. It lists object-ID conflicts, missing agreed photo views and condition statements needing a conservator, with actual source pointers. It does not diagnose damage or assign liability. Its value depends on records retained outside an already integrated collections system.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** CONDITIONAL, 10–16 synthetic reports/photos, one object plus decoy loan; prove ID and view mapping first.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** 0:00–0:30 loan handoff; 0:30–1:30 similar decoy object and missing reverse view; 1:30–2:20 “unchanged” statement conflicts with observation; 2:20–3:00 conservator escalation with pointers. Not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH search/image description; MEDIUM full loan-review job.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** MEDIUM; inspection is a predictable extension, with mature vertical products.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Less obvious unseeded, easy from museum context; not LOW.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “Articheck already does this; VLM cannot assess damage.”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Documented professional boundary and vivid identity/completeness failures without physical intervention.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Existing Articheck/EMu already resolves target handoff, source/object mapping fails, or users require automatic damage diagnosis.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W04 — ConsentBoundary: oral-history deposit preflight\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Oral-history archivist / accession coordinator [S20–S22].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Reconcile interview identity, release versions, narrator review and restrictions before deposit.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Recordings, transcripts, release forms, contextual records and restrictions [S20–S22].\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — FACT:** OHA archive manual explicitly discusses shared-server/cloud workflows [S21]. INFERENCE: N2, not NAS-brand prevalence.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Preserve consent/context alongside media; institutions define specific use permissions [S20–S22], not universal law.\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Findable audio has absent, superseded or wrong-interview authorization.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Deposit delay, restricted reuse and unwanted exposure; not automated legal compliance.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** OHMS and Aviary [S23–S24], integrated adjacent tools, not independent vendors.\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Metadata/indexing/synchronization and searchable media presentation/access controls [S23–S24].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Legacy local release/restriction reconciliation before platform ingest.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Same files suffice for generic reasoning; repeatable pre-deposit review over local originals must add actual value.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Review long-lived audio and consent files before publishing-system ingest; network locality still UNKNOWN.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Interview/date correspondence, incompatible restrictions, absent review evidence and superseded forms; not consent inferred from silence.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Archivist/rights holder interprets consent and approves access/deposit. Never an ACL/publication agent.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central; release version and real passage/segment locations.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Necessary when spoken restriction conflicts with written form; otherwise documents may suffice. ASR alone establishes no consent.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Per-file document review, audio transcription, evidence collection and saved text [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 + ASR restriction/speaker localization + release-version mapping = **8 groups**. Unsupported: ACL, publishing, e-signatures, narrator notifications.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** ConsentBoundary helps oral-history archivists prepare a deposit without confusing searchable media with permission to use it. It checks selected interview files, releases and restriction notes, reporting conflicts and missing evidence with source pointers. An archivist resolves every access decision. It neither changes permissions nor publishes material.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** CONDITIONAL, 10–18 synthetic files, two releases and two short audio excerpts. If restriction attribution fails, the audio-dependent concept fails; document-only reduction is separate.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** 0:00–0:30 deposit; 0:30–1:30 later embargo versus earlier release; 1:30–2:20 missing release for another recording; 2:20–3:00 unresolved archivist review, no permission changes. Not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH retrieval/transcription; MEDIUM pre-deposit restriction reconciliation.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** MEDIUM; consent/compliance assistants remain predictable.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy with oral-history context, not novel by name.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “You cannot enforce consent; Aviary already has permissions.”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Pre-deposit consistency is distinct from enforcement; explicit shared-server practice and professional guidance.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Value requires enforcement/publishing, precedence is unavailable, or ASR fabricates attribution/locations.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W05 — CaseBinder—TradeCloseout: installed-equipment handoff\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Trade subcontractor closeout coordinator [S25–S26].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Check one installed-equipment package against owner-approved requirements before handover.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Manuals, warranties, submittals and change records [S25–S26]. ASSUMPTION: Plate photos/readable exports are available.\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — INFERENCE:** N1: contractor project exports plausibly use file shares; sources do not establish NAS prevalence. All-Procore firms may lack this need.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Contract-specific binders and changes are reconciled using project workflows [S25–S27].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Every document type exists but the manual/model, warranty or revision is wrong for the installed asset.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Resubmission, handover delay and maintenance confusion; no invented payment-delay figure.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Procore and Autodesk Pype / Forma Operations [S25,S27].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Pype extracts requirements, reminds, collects/packages and handles specification versions; Procore offers submittal/action-plan workflows [S25,S27].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Installed-ID discrepancies across supplier exports, plate photos and changed requirements; not evidence Pype cannot do it.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** A generic model with same packet/checklist can reason similarly; local repeatability and reliable coverage must justify use.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Only material for contractors already retaining originals on NAS; image/document review without moving a full archive.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Required item→installed asset→matching approved document; contradictions and missing support, not engineering adequacy.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Trade lead/owner controls requirements, revision precedence and acceptance; no payment release/certification.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central; requirement and actual installed-ID support for each exception.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Plate-photo versus document-ID is genuine; PDF-only completeness is a weaker crowded reduction.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Photo OCR/meta, per-file document QA/reconcile and evidence text output [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 + equipment-ID normalization = **7 groups**. Unsupported: BIM/CAD, email/reminders, PM/ERP APIs, automatic acceptance.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** CaseBinder TradeCloseout checks a selected equipment handoff against an approved checklist. It connects installed nameplate evidence to manuals, warranties and approved revisions, listing mismatches and missing support for human review. It is not project management. Pype and Procore already automate closeout; cross-export discrepancy handling must earn its place.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** CONDITIONAL, 12–24 synthetic files/two assets, wrong-model manual and changed checklist; prove OCR and text path first.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** 0:00–0:30 apparently complete folder; 0:30–1:30 wrong model; 1:30–2:20 obsolete revision and absent training evidence; 2:20–3:00 human review artifact. Not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH summary/checklist substeps; MEDIUM full installed-ID reconciliation.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH completeness/compliance binder, compounded by mature vertical automation.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy; trade label and provenance do not lower collision.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “Pype already automates closeout; why NAS?”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Concrete deliverable, objective mismatches and relatively simple bounded inputs.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Work already fully in Procore/Pype, originals incidental to NAS, or BIM/integration necessary for value.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W06 — DesignDelta: drawing/RFI revision review\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Architect/design coordinator comparing drawings [S28].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Determine which issued changes affect unresolved RFIs before release.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** PDF drawings/markups [S28]. ASSUMPTION: RFI text/issue registers accompany them.\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — INFERENCE:** N1 shared project sets plausible, prevalence unverified.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Bluebeam Compare/Overlay highlights drawing changes [S28].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Geometric change contradicts an old approval while filenames appear current.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Re-review/coordination error; no quantified construction loss.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Bluebeam Revu; adjacent Autodesk construction review [S26,S28].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Graphical comparisons/overlays and project review workflows [S26,S28].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Geometric change→RFI meaning still needs specialist review; no new market gap established.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Generic multimodal models may help; text-only comparisons are not equivalent to geometry.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Storage cannot supply the missing core operation.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Geometry/obligation relationship, not filename or text revision alone.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Design professionals approve meaning/issue status.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central drawing revision/location and approval record.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Essential geometric/visual task, not satisfied by OCR.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Registers may be readable; drawing overlay/render/geometry is not established [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — FACT (catalog-bounded): HARD_BLOCKER** for intended geometric comparison. UNKNOWN G1–G6; no custom CAD engine.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** DesignDelta would reconcile issued drawing changes with unresolved RFIs. Reliable geometric comparison is central, and the public Skill catalog does not expose it. A text-only revision checklist would be a different reduced topic. The full workflow should not advance by hiding that limitation.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** Not feasible as defined; no sanctioned geometry operation established.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** An overlay would imply unsupported functionality; no feasible full-scope demo asserted or built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH text/revision summary; geometry is a separate specialist market.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH design-document assistant.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy revision-assistant idea.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “Where is the actual drawing comparison?”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Real consequential review problem, outweighed by blocker.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Reject original scope absent sanctioned geometry capability; text diff is not a substitute.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W07 — ClaimPacket: property-loss evidence preparation\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Policyholder/restoration documentation coordinator [S42–S43].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Link item claims to photos/inventory/receipts for human review.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Inventory, claim records, field photos/notes/reports [S42–S43].\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — INFERENCE:** N1 weak; phone/cloud capture may dominate, no essential NAS proven.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Maintain records; Encircle aggregates field documentation/reports [S42–S43].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Duplicate identities, absent receipt or inconsistent dates.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Clarification/resubmission; no coverage/settlement prediction.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Encircle; adjacent general multimodal AI [S43,S06–S08].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Field photos/video/notes, carrier-oriented reports and AI features [S43].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Old private records outside field tools; weak evidence of NAS-native gap.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Same small packet easily uploaded to a strong multimodal model; no exclusive reasoning advantage.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Conditional on substantial pre-loss local history; otherwise a generic binder is simpler.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Item/date consistency and support, never causation, value or entitlement.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Claimant/professional decides content; insurer decides coverage/submission outcome.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central receipt/item/photo correspondence.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Genuine photos/receipts, already served by restoration tools.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Photo OCR/VLM and document reconciliation for tiny precheck [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 = **6 groups**. Unsupported: insurer API, valuation engine or auto-submission.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** ClaimPacket would check selected loss files for item mismatches and missing evidence. It would not assess coverage or value. Existing restoration tools already generate sophisticated reports, and a NAS-specific advantage is weakly evidenced.
